% ---- labels ----
\node[slabel, anchor=west] (lc) at (8.2,5.7) {cherubims of beaten gold, their wings\\covering, their faces toward each other\\and toward the propitiatory};
\draw[line width=0.2pt, black!70] (lc.west) -- (5.0,5.8);
\node[slabel, anchor=west] (lp) at (8.2,4.35) {the propitiatory,\\of the purest gold};
\draw[line width=0.2pt, black!70] (lp.west) -- (6.55,4.12);
\node[slabel, anchor=west] (lk) at (8.2,3.1) {``a golden crown round about''\\(Ex 25:11)};
\draw[line width=0.2pt, black!70] (lk.west) -- (6.4,3.6);
\node[slabel, anchor=west] (lw) at (8.2,2.0) {setim wood, overlaid with gold\\within and without};
\draw[line width=0.2pt, black!70] (lw.west) -- (6.4,2.2);
\node[slabel, anchor=west] (ls) at (8.2,0.9) {the staves, always in the rings};
\draw[line width=0.2pt, black!70] (ls.west) -- (7.55,1.3);
\end{tikzpicture}
\sketchcaption{The ark as commanded. The measures are Scripture's (Exodus
25:10--22, Douay wording in the labels); the forms are not --- Scripture
nowhere describes the cherubims' shape, and this reconstruction is editorial
conjecture, drawn to fix the parts and their names, not the look.}
\end{sketchfig}
